k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},0,0])}}{([q_{c}!,0!,0!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{d},0])}}{([q_{d}!,0!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},0])}}{([q_{c}!,0!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}=

(89)

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([0,q_{d},q_{1}])}}{([0!,q_{d}!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},0,q_{1}])}}{([q_{c}!,0!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{d},q_{1}])}}{([q_{d}!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},q_{1}])}}{([q_{c}!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}.

5.2
GMOI Lipschitz Estimations

In this section, we will apply Theorem
2
to determine GMOI Lipschitz estimations. The Lipschitz estimation for GMOI is to determine the norm for the difference between GMOI
T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})
and GMOI
T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{\zeta})
is terms of norms for the difference
\bm{Y}_{i}
and
\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{i}
. Theorem
4
below is provided to characterize GMOI Lipschitz estimations.

Theorem 4

We have Lipschitz estimation for the difference between GMOI
T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})
and GMOI
T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{\zeta})
:

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})-T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{\zeta})\right\|

(90)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\zeta}\Upsilon_{i}(\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1},\beta)\left(\prod\limits_{j=1}^{i-1}\left\|\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{j}\right\|\right)\left\|\bm{Y}_{i}-\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{i}\right\|\left(\prod\limits_{j=i+1}^{\zeta}\left\|\bm{Y}_{j}\right\|\right),

where
\Upsilon_{i}(\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1},\beta)
is the scalar component of the upper bound for the following GMOI expressed by

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}^{\prime}]_{1}^{i-1},\bm{Y}_{i}-\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{i},[\bm{Y}]_{i+1}^{\zeta})\right\|

(91)

\displaystyle\leq_{1}

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i^{\prime}=0}^{2^{\zeta+1}-1}\left\|\bm{A}_{i^{\prime}}\right\|_{up}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{i}(\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1},\beta)\left(\prod\limits_{j=1}^{i-1}\left\|\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{j}\right\|\right)\left\|\bm{Y}_{i}-\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{i}\right\|\left(\prod\limits_{j=i+1}^{\zeta}\left\|\bm{Y}_{j}\right\|\right).

The inequality
\leq_{1}
comes from Theorem
2
.

Proof:

We can perform a telescoping decomposition for the L.H.S. of Eq. (
90
) as

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})-T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{\zeta})\right\|

(92)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\zeta}\left(T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}^{\prime}]_{1}^{i-1},[\bm{Y}]_{i}^{\zeta})-T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}^{\prime}]_{1}^{i},[\bm{Y}]_{i+1}^{\zeta})\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\zeta}T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}^{\prime}]_{1}^{i-1},\bm{Y}_{i}-\bm{Y}^{\prime}_{i},[\bm{Y}]_{i+1}^{\zeta})\right\|.

Then, this theorem is proved by applying Theorem
2
to obtain
\Upsilon_{i}(\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1},\beta)
given by Eq. (
91
).

\hfill\Box

6
GMOI Continuity

The purpose of this section is to establish the continuity property of GMOI, i.e., we will have

\displaystyle T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1,\ell_{1}},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1,\ell_{\zeta+1}}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})\rightarrow T_{\beta}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta}),

(93)

given that

\displaystyle\bm{X}_{i,\ell_{i}}\rightarrow\bm{X}_{i}.

(94)

Note that
\rightarrow
is in the sense of Frobenius norm.

The following Lemma
3
has to be built before GMOI continuity property establishment.

Lemma 3

Given
\bm{C}\rightarrow\bm{D}
, we have

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{X}}([\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{C},\bm{D},[\bm{X}]_{j}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\bm{O},

(95)

where
\bm{O}
is a matrix with all entries as zeros.

Proof:

From Eq. (
61
), we have

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{X}}([\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{C},\bm{D},[\bm{X}]_{j}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,P}}

(96)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,N}}+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,P}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,N}}+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,N}}.

Claim 1:
\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}\rightarrow\bm{O}

Let
\bm{C}=\bm{D}+t\bm{V}
, where
t\in\mathbb{R}
and
\bm{V}
is a fixed perturbation matrix. Then under smooth variation of the Jordan decomposition (i.e., assuming stable geometric multiplicities), we have:

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}=O(t)\quad\text{as}\quad t\to 0.

We recall the expression:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}S_{k_{p},i_{p}},p\in[1,j-2],\\
S_{k_{p^{\prime}},i_{p^{\prime}}},p^{\prime}\in[j+1,\zeta]\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{j-1},i_{j-1},\\
k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d}\\
k_{j},i_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(\beta^{[\zeta+1]}([\lambda_{k}]_{1}^{j-2},\lambda_{k_{j-1}},\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{j}},[\lambda_{k}]_{j+1}^{\zeta})\right)^{([q]_{1}^{j-2}[0,0,0,0][q]_{j+1}^{\zeta})}}{([q!]_{1}^{j-2}[0!,0!,0!,0!][q!]_{j+1}^{\zeta})}

\displaystyle\times\left(\prod_{p=1}^{j-2}S_{k_{p},i_{p}}\bm{Y}_{p}\right)\bm{P}_{k_{j-1},i_{j-1}}\bm{Y}_{j-1}\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}_{j}\bm{P}_{k_{j},i_{j}}\left(\prod_{p^{\prime}=j+1}^{\zeta}\bm{Y}_{p^{\prime}}S_{k_{p^{\prime}},i_{p^{\prime}}}\right).

(97)

We now study the behavior of the central difference:

\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{C})-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(\bm{D}),

where
\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{C})
denotes the nilpotent part of the matrix
\bm{C}
corresponding to the
k_{c}
-th eigenvalue and the
i_{c}
-th geometric component, with exponent
q_{c}
. Similarly,
\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(\bm{D})
denotes the nilpotent part of the matrix
\bm{D}
corresponding to the
k_{d}
-th eigenvalue and the
i_{d}
-th geometric component, with exponent
q_{d}
.

Let
\bm{C}=\bm{D}+t\bm{V}
and suppose the eigenvalues of
\bm{C}
and
\bm{D}
satisfy

\lambda_{k_{c}}=\lambda_{k_{d}}+t\mu+o(t),

with corresponding Jordan subspaces continuously varying. Then by perturbation theory for Jordan blocks:

\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{C})=\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(\bm{D})+t\cdot\dot{\bm{N}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{c}}+o(t),

and

\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{C})=\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(\bm{D})+t\cdot\dot{\bm{P}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}+o(t),

where
\dot{\bm{N}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}
and
\dot{\bm{P}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}
are Fréchet derivatives with respect to
t
.

Thus, the difference term becomes:

\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}=t\cdot\left(\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\dot{\bm{N}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{c}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\right)+o(t).

Substituting this into the full sum, each term is of the form:

t\cdot\left(\text{bounded coefficient involving }\beta^{[\zeta+1]}\text{ and }\bm{Y}_{p},\bm{P}_{k,i},\bm{S}_{k,i}\right)+o(t),

which implies:

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}=O(t)\quad\text{as }t\to 0.

Claim 2:
\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,P}}\rightarrow\bm{O}

Let
\bm{C}=\bm{D}+t\bm{V}
for small
t>0
and fixed matrix
\bm{V}
, and suppose the Jordan decomposition of
\bm{C}
depends smoothly on
t
. Then:

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,P}}=O(t)\quad\text{as}\quad t\to 0.

The expression for
\mathfrak{X}
consists of four multilinear terms in Eq. (
63
), each involving differences between nilpotent components of
\bm{C}
and
\bm{D}
.

Again, by perturbation theory for Jordan blocks:

\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{C})=\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(\bm{D})+t\cdot\dot{\bm{N}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{c}}+o(t),

and

\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{C})=\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(\bm{D})+t\cdot\dot{\bm{P}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}+o(t),

where
\dot{\bm{N}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}
and
\dot{\bm{P}}_{k_{d},i_{d}}
are Fréchet derivatives with respect to
t
.

Now analyze each of the four terms in Eq. (
63
):

Term 1:

\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}=O(t),

since
(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})=O(t)
and all other matrices (e.g.,
\bm{Y}_{j}
) are fixed.

Term 2:

\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}=O(t),

because
(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})=O(t)
is again an
O(t)
difference.

We prove that the third and fourth terms in Eq. (
63
) cancel in the limit
\bm{C}\to\bm{D}
. Denote the third term as
T_{3}
and the fourth term as
T_{4}
. Then, we have:

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}S_{k_{p},i_{p}},p\in[1,j-2],\\
S_{k_{p^{\prime}},i_{p^{\prime}}},p^{\prime}\in[j+1,\zeta]\end{subarray}}\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{j-1},i_{j-1},\\
k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d}\\
k_{j},i_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[\zeta]}([\lambda_{k}]_{1}^{j-2},\lambda_{k_{j-1}},\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{j}},[\lambda_{k}]_{j+1}^{\zeta}))^{([q]_{1}^{j-2}[0,q_{d},0][q]_{j+1}^{\zeta})}}{([q!]_{1}^{j-2}[0!,q_{d}!,0!][q!]_{j+1}^{\zeta})}

(98)

\displaystyle\times\left(\prod\limits_{p=1}^{j-2}S_{k_{p},i_{p}}\bm{Y}_{p}\right)\bm{P}_{k_{j-1},i_{j-1}}\bm{Y}_{j-1}\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}_{j}\bm{P}_{k_{j},i_{j}}\left(\prod\limits_{p^{\prime}=j+1}^{\zeta}\bm{Y}_{p^{\prime}}S_{k_{p^{\prime}},i_{p^{\prime}}}\right),

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}S_{k_{p},i_{p}},p\in[1,j-2],\\
S_{k_{p^{\prime}},i_{p^{\prime}}},p^{\prime}\in[j+1,\zeta]\end{subarray}}\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{j-1},i_{j-1},\\
k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d}\\
k_{j},i_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[\zeta]}([\lambda_{k}]_{1}^{j-2},\lambda_{k_{j-1}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{j}},[\lambda_{k}]_{j+1}^{\zeta}))^{([q]_{1}^{j-2}[0,q_{c},0][q]_{j+1}^{\zeta})}}{([q!]_{1}^{j-2}[0!,q_{c}!,0!][q!]_{j+1}^{\zeta})}

(99)

\displaystyle\times\left(\prod\limits_{p=1}^{j-2}S_{k_{p},i_{p}}\bm{Y}_{p}\right)\bm{P}_{k_{j-1},i_{j-1}}\bm{Y}_{j-1}\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}_{j}\bm{P}_{k_{j},i_{j}}\left(\prod\limits_{p^{\prime}=j+1}^{\zeta}\bm{Y}_{p^{\prime}}S_{k_{p^{\prime}},i_{p^{\prime}}}\right)

Now, observe the following:

1.

As
\bm{C}\to\bm{D}
, we have
\lambda_{k_{c}}\to\lambda_{k_{d}}
and the generalized divided differences
\beta^{[\zeta]}
converge:

\beta^{[\zeta]}(\cdots,\lambda_{k_{c}},\cdots)\to\beta^{[\zeta]}(\cdots,\lambda_{k_{d}},\cdots).

2.

The projectors and nilpotents satisfy:

\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\to\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}},\quad\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\to\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}.

3.

Furthermore, in the Jordan canonical basis, the nilpotent and projector commute:

\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}=\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}.

Combining these, we find that in the limit
\bm{C}\to\bm{D}
:

T_{3}\to\sum\beta(\cdots)\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}},\qquad T_{4}\to\sum\beta(\cdots)\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}.

Since these matrices commute in the limit and the coefficients match, we conclude:

T_{3}-T_{4}\to\bm{0}.

Therefore, combining the asymptotic bounds and noting all differences are
O(t)
, we conclude:

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,P}}=O(t)\to\bm{O}\quad\text{as }\bm{C}\to\bm{D}.

This lemma can be proved by using the same methods from proving Claim 1 and Claim 2 to show the following

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,N}}\rightarrow\bm{O},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,N}}\rightarrow\bm{O},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,P}}\rightarrow\bm{O},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,P}}\rightarrow\bm{O},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{j,N}}\rightarrow\bm{O},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{j,N}}\rightarrow\bm{O}.

(100)

\hfill\Box

We are ready to present Theorem
5
, which is used to establish the GMOI continuity property.

Theorem 5

Suppose that

\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}\rightarrow\bm{X}_{j},

(101)

for all
j=1,2,\ldots,\zeta+1
, and assume that all partial derivatives of the function
\beta^{[\zeta+1]}
up to any order are bounded. Then, it follows that

\displaystyle T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\bm{X}_{1,\ell_{1}},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1,\ell_{\zeta+1}}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})\rightarrow T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta}).

(102)

Proof:

By telescoping summation formula, we have

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\bm{X}_{1,\ell_{1}},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1,\ell_{\zeta+1}}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\bm{X}_{1},\ldots,\bm{X}_{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})\right\|

(103)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\zeta+1}\left(T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j}^{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})\right)\right\|.

For each index
j
, by applying GMOI perturbation formula given by Theorem
3
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j}^{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1}}(\bm{Y}_{1},\ldots,\bm{Y}_{\zeta}\right\|

(104)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}},\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})-\bar{\mathfrak{X}}([\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}},\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1})\right.

\displaystyle-\left.T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},P},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+2}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right.

\displaystyle-\left.T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},N},[\bm{X}]_{j+2}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right.

\displaystyle-\left.T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},P},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+2}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right.

\displaystyle-\left.T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},N},[\bm{X}]_{j+2}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}},\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+1}^{\zeta}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right\|+

\displaystyle+\left\|\bar{\mathfrak{X}}([\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}},\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1})\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},P},[\bm{X}_{\ell}]_{j+2}^{\zeta}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,P},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},N},[\bm{X}]_{j+2}^{\zeta}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},P},[\bm{X}]_{j+2}^{\zeta}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-2},\bm{X}_{j-1,N},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j},N},\bm{X}_{j,N},\bm{X}_{j+1,\ell_{j+1},N},[\bm{X}]_{j+2}^{\zeta}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{j,\ell_{j}}-\bm{X}_{j},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq_{1}

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{6}+\left(\frac{\epsilon}{6}\right)_{\star}+\frac{\epsilon}{6}+\frac{\epsilon}{6}+\frac{\epsilon}{6}+\frac{\epsilon}{6}=\epsilon,

where each
\frac{\epsilon}{6}
without
\star
comes from Theorem
2
and the assumption that all partial derivatives of the function
\beta^{[\zeta+1]}
up to any order are bounded. Besides, the term
(\frac{\epsilon}{6})_{\star}
comes from Lemma
3
.

Then, this theorem is proved because each summand in Eq. (
103
) approaches to zero from Eq. (
104
).

\hfill\Box

7
Applications: Differentiation of Matrix Functions

The purpose of this section is to apply previous GMOI theory to study the differentiation of a matrix function. The goal is to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt^{n}},

(105)

where
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Recall Theorem 11
[
9
]
, we have

Theorem 6

Let
f
be a complex-valued and differentiable function, we have

\displaystyle\frac{df(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{f^{[1]}}^{\bm{X}+t\bm{Y},\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}}\left(\bm{Y}\right),

(106)

where
T_{f^{[1]}}^{\bm{X}+t\bm{Y},\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}}\left(\bm{Y}\right)
is the GDOI with matrices
\bm{X}
and
\bm{Y}
.

Before presenting the main theorem in this section, we have to introduce the following lemmas. Lemma
4
shows tha the differentiation of a GMOI will be a zero matrix if all parameter matrices are nilpotent parts of the original parameter matrices.

Lemma 4

Given a GMOI with the following format:

\displaystyle T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta}),

(107)

we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}=\bm{O}.

(108)

Proof:

By derivative definition and telescoping summation relation, we have

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})}{t}=

(109)

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\zeta+1}\left(T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j-1},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})\right)}{t}.

Then, for each
j
in the summand of the numerator of Eq. (
109
), we have

\displaystyle T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j-1},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})

(110)

\displaystyle=_{1}

\displaystyle T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j-1},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},t\bm{Y},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j-1},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{j-1},t\bm{Y},[\bm{Y}]_{j}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-\overline{\mathfrak{X}}([\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j-1},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j}^{\zeta+1})

\displaystyle=_{2}

\displaystyle\bm{O},

where the perturbation formula given by Theorem
3
is applied to get
=_{1}
, and Eq. (
61
) is used to get
=_{2}
since
\overline{\mathfrak{X}}([\bm{X}_{N}]_{1}^{j-1},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]_{j}^{\zeta+1})=\bm{O}
by setting
\bm{C}=(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}
and
\bm{D}=\bm{X}_{N}
, respectively.

\hfill\Box

Lemma
5
will study the the differentiation of a GMOI given that not all parameter matrices are nilpotent parts of the original parameter matrices.

Lemma 5

Given a GMOI with the following format:

\displaystyle T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta}),

(111)

where
\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}
is an array of parameter matrices with
\zeta+1
matrices composed by at least one matrix
\bm{X}
and at least one matrix
\bm{X}_{N}
, and the subscript
\ell
is used to index those many possible arrays of parameter matrices with
\zeta+1
matrices composed by at least one matrix
\bm{X}
and at least one matrix
\bm{X}_{N}
. We define the mapping function
\vartheta_{\ell}(j)
as the position of the
j
-th paramter matrix
\bm{X}
in the array
\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}
, where
j=1,2,\ldots,\left|\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}\right|_{\bm{X}}
and
\left|\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}\right|_{\bm{X}}
represents the number of
\bm{X}
in the array of parameter matrices
\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}
.
Let
[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1}
represents the first
\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1
parameter matrices of the array
\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}
, and
[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}
represents the last
\zeta-\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1
parameter matrices of the array
\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}
. We also use
\tilde{\bm{X}}=\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}
for later notations simplification.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\underline{[\bm{X}+t\bm{Y},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]}_{\ell}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

(112)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\left|\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}\right|_{\bm{X}}}\Bigg[T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\bm{X},\bm{X},[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\bm{Y},[\bm{Y}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\bm{Y},[\bm{Y}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}\left([\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}\right)\right|_{t=0}\Bigg]

where
\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}\left([\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}\right)\right|_{t=0}
is obtained by

\displaystyle\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}\left([\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}\right)\right|_{t=0}

(113)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d\mathfrak{X}\left([\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}\right)}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

Proof:

From Lemma
4
, we only need to consider those terms involving
\bm{X}
in the telescoping summation decomposition for
T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\underline{[\bm{X}+t\bm{Y},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]}_{\ell}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})
. As derivative for GMOIs involving
\bm{X}_{N}
will be zero matrices.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\underline{[\bm{X}+t\bm{Y},(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})_{N}]}_{\ell}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\zeta})}{t}=

(114)

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{1}{t}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\left|\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}\right|_{\bm{X}}}\Bigg[T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},[\bm{Y}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-T_{\beta^{[\zeta]}}^{[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},[\bm{Y}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)}^{\zeta})\Bigg].

Again, by the perturbation formula given by Theorem
3
, we obtain

R.H.S. of Eq. (
114
)

(115)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{1}{t}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\left|\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}\right|_{\bm{X}}}\Bigg[T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},t\bm{Y},[\bm{Y}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-T_{\beta^{[\zeta+1]}}^{[\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},t\bm{Y},[\bm{Y}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)}^{\zeta})

\displaystyle-\mathfrak{X}\left([\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}\right)\Bigg].

This lemma is proved by setting
t=0
in Eq. (
115
).

\hfill\Box

The exact computation of
\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}\left([\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{1}^{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell}]_{\vartheta_{\ell}(j)+1}^{\zeta+1}\right)\right|_{t=0}
is tedious and there is no simple formula to express this exactly due to various combination of entries in the array
[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{\ell}
. But, we will provide an example to show how to calculate this.

We are ready to present the main Theorem
7
in this section about the representing
n
-th derivative of a matrix-valued function in terms of GMOIs.

Theorem 7

Given any matrices
\bm{X}
and
\bm{Y}
, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{n}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt^{n}}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{n+1}}_{f^{[n]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n})-(n-1)!\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}T_{f^{[n]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[\bm{X}]_{j+1}^{n}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n})

(116)

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-1}(n-i)!\left.\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-i+1}\mathfrak{X}^{(i)}([\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\tilde{\bm{X}}]_{j+1}^{n-i+1})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{\ell_{n-1}=1}^{\left(\sum\limits_{k=1}^{\lceil\frac{n-1}{2}\rceil}\frac{(n-k)!}{k!(n-2k)!}\right)-1(\mbox{\penalty\ if $n$ is even})}a_{\ell_{n-1}}\left.\frac{dT_{f^{[n-1]}}^{\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell_{n-1}}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n-1})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+\left[\sum\limits_{\rho=4}^{n}\sum\limits_{i_{\rho}=1}^{\gamma(\rho)}b_{i_{\rho}}\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(n+2-\rho)}([\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{i_{\rho}})\right|_{t=0}\right]\bm{1}(\mbox{if $n\geq 4$}),

where
n\geq 2
,
a_{\ell_{n-1}}
are coefficients for those GMOIs with parameters matrices containing both
\bm{X}
and
\bm{X}_{N}
from
\frac{d^{n-1}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt^{n-1}}
,
b_{i_{\rho}}
are coefficients for those
\mathfrak{X}^{(k)}([\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{i_{\rho}})
from
\frac{d^{n-1}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt^{n-1}}
with
k=1,2,\ldots,n-3
, and
[\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{i_{\rho}}
is the array indexed by
i_{\rho}
composed by
\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X}
and
\bm{X}_{N}
, respectively with length
\rho
. The term
\gamma(\rho)
is determined by

\displaystyle\gamma(\rho)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\frac{(\rho-2)!}{1!(\rho-3)!},\frac{(\rho-3)!}{2!(\rho-5)!},\ldots,1\right]\cdot[\rho-3,\rho-5,\ldots,0],

(117)

where
\cdot
is the inner product operation. Note that
\bm{1}(\mbox{if $n\geq 4$})
is the indicator condition function.

Proof:

Becuase we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{df(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{2}}_{f^{[1]}}([\bm{Y}]),

(118)

by applying Theorem
3
to Eq.
118
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{2}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{2}t}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{T^{[\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2}}_{f^{[1]}}([\bm{Y}])-T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{2}}_{f^{[1]}}([\bm{Y}])}{t}

(119)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{3}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})-T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}.

By taking the derivative of
f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})
repeatdely and applying Theorem
3
to every terms except those GMOIs with parameter matrices conposed by
\bm{X}
and
\bm{X}_{N}
jointly, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{n}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt^{n}}\right|_{t=0},\mbox{without $T^{\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}}$ derivatives}

(120)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{n+1}}_{f^{[n]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n})-(n-1)!\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}T_{f^{[n]}}^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},[\bm{X}]_{j+1}^{n}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-1}(n-i)!\left.\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-i+1}\mathfrak{X}^{(i)}([\bm{X}]_{1}^{j-1},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},[\tilde{\bm{X}}]_{j+1}^{n-i+1})\right|_{t=0}.

For those derivatives of GMOIs involving
T^{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}
, there are
\left(\sum\limits_{k=1}^{\lceil\frac{n-1}{2}\rceil}\frac{(n-k)!}{k!(n-2k)!}\right)
terms of GMOIs with parameter matrices having
\bm{X}_{N}
. However, from Lemma
4
, we only need to consider GMOIs with parameter matrices composed by
\bm{X}
and
\bm{X}_{N}
jointly. Therefore, we need to reduce one in the total number of
\ell_{n-1}
. By Lemma
5
, we can evaluate each term of
\left.\frac{dT_{f^{[n-1]}}^{\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell_{n-1}}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n-1})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}
. Because there are
\gamma(\rho)
terms for each
i_{\rho}
to form
\mathfrak{X}^{(n+2-\rho)}([\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{i_{\rho}})
when we apply perturbation formula from Theorem
3
in evaluating
\frac{dT_{f^{[n-1]}}^{\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell_{n-1}}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n-1})}{dt}
.

Let us explain why the value
\gamma(\rho)
can be expressed by

\displaystyle\gamma(\rho)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\frac{(\rho-2)!}{1!(\rho-3)!},\frac{(\rho-3)!}{2!(\rho-5)!},\ldots,1\right]\cdot[\rho-3,\rho-5,\ldots,0].

(121)

We obtain

\frac{(\rho-k-1)!}{k!(\rho-2k-1)!}

GMOIs of parameter matrices with the length
\rho-1
from the derivative

\frac{d^{n-2}}{dt^{n-2}}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}),

since we begin considering the operator derivative structure of

\mathfrak{X}([\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{i_{\rho}})

from order 2. Moreover, each such GMOI includes
\rho-2k-1
instances of
\tilde{\bm{X}}
. As a result, we have

\frac{(\rho-k-1)!}{k!(\rho-2k-1)!}\times(\rho-2k-1)

terms of the form

\mathfrak{X}([\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}])
,
where the total array length is
\rho
, and there are exactly
k
pairs of
\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}
. The total count

\gamma(\rho)
is then obtained by summing over all admissible values of
k
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{n}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{dt^{n}}\right|_{t=0},\mbox{with $T^{\underline{[\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]}}$ derivatives}

(122)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{\ell_{n-1}=1}^{\left(\sum\limits_{k=1}^{\lceil\frac{n-1}{2}\rceil}\frac{(n-k)!}{k!(n-2k)!}\right)-1(\mbox{\penalty\ if $n$ is even})}a_{\ell_{n-1}}\left.\frac{dT_{f^{[n-1]}}^{\underline{[\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}]}_{\ell_{n-1}}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{n-1})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\rho=4}^{n}\sum\limits_{i_{\rho}=1}^{\gamma(\rho)}b_{i_{\rho}}\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(n+2-\rho)}([\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N}]_{i_{\rho}})\right|_{t=0}.

This theorem is proved by combing Eq. (
120
) and Eq. (
122
)

\hfill\Box

Below, we will present examples to illustrate
\left.\frac{d^{3}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{3}t}\right|_{t=0}
and
\left.\frac{d^{4}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{4}t}\right|_{t=0}
.

Example 4

Since we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{2}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{2}t}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow 0}\frac{T^{[\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2}}_{f^{[1]}}([\bm{Y}])-T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{2}}_{f^{[1]}}([\bm{Y}])}{t}

(123)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{3}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})-T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0},

by the telescoping summation and the perturbation formula given by Theorem
3
, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d2T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{3}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{4}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})-2T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

(124)

\displaystyle-2T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})-2T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

\displaystyle-2\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})-2\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})

\displaystyle-2\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X}).

By applying Lemma
5
to
T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})
and
T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

(125)

\displaystyle-\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N}),

and

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[2]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{2})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

(126)

\displaystyle-\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X}).

By combining Eq. (
124
) to Eq. (
126
), we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{3}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{3}t}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{4}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})-3T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

\displaystyle-2T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})-3T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

\displaystyle-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}+2T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|_{t=0}+\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

For the evaluation of
\left.\frac{d^{4}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{4}t}\right|_{t=0}
, we first derive

\displaystyle 3!\frac{dT^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{4}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{5}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-3!T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

(128)

\displaystyle-3!T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-3!T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-3!T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-3!\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})-3!\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})

\displaystyle-3!\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})-3!\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X}).

by the telescoping summation and the perturbation formula given by Theorem
3
.

We also have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

(129)

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0},

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

(130)

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0},

and

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dT^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[3]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{3})}{dt}\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

(131)

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|.

By combining Eq. (
128
) to Eq. (
131
), we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{4}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{4}t}\right|_{t=0}

(132)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{5}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-3!T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-3!T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-3!T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-3!T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-3\Bigg(T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-2\Bigg(T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-3\Bigg(T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|_{t=0}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(3)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(3)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|_{t=0}+\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}.

After arranging terms, we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d^{4}f(\bm{X}+t\bm{Y})}{d^{4}t}\right|_{t=0}

(133)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4!T^{[\bm{X}]_{1}^{5}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-12T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-8T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-8T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})-12T^{\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-3!\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle-2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(3)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}-\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(3)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+3\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}+3\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}+5T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})

\displaystyle+2\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}+6T^{\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})+5\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+5T^{\bm{X},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N}}_{f^{[4]}}([\bm{Y}]_{1}^{4})+3\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(1)}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|_{t=0}

\displaystyle+\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{N})\right|_{t=0}+\left.\mathfrak{X}^{(2)}(\bm{X}_{N},\bm{X}_{N},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})\right|_{t=0}.

From Example
4
, we need to evaluate high-order derivatives of terms involving
\mathfrak{X}
. In following Example
5
, we will try to evaluate any
n
-th order derivative of
\mathfrak{X}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})
. For other evaluation of high-order derivatives of terms involving
\mathfrak{X}
, they can be calculated similarly although more complicated.

Example 5

By Eq. (
85
), the term
\mathfrak{X}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})
can be expressed by

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{X}}(\bm{C},\bm{D},\bm{X}_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}}

(134)

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}+\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}},

where
\bm{C}=\bm{X},\bm{D}=\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}
and
\bm{X}_{1}=\bm{X}
. Then, the
n
-th derivative of
\mathfrak{X}(\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{X}},\bm{X})
can be expressed by

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\bar{\mathfrak{X}}(\bm{C},\bm{D},\bm{X}_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}+\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}}

(135)

\displaystyle+\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}+\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}.

In the following, we will evaluate
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}
,
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}}
,
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}
and
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}
, respectively.

The Evaluation of
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}

Because we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}=

(136)

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d}\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([0,0,0])}}{([0!,0!,0!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}},

to derive the
n
-th derivative of the operator-valued expression

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}

with respect to
t
, from Eq. (
136
), we need to analyze how each term behaves under differentiation when the matrix
\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}
appears implicitly in the spectral data (
\lambda_{k_{d}}
,
\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}
, and
\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}
).

Differentiating
\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}(t)
with respect to
t
, the only
t
-dependence comes from:

•

\lambda_{k_{d}}(t)
,

•

\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)
,

•

\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(t)
.

Then, the
n
-th derivative becomes:

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}(t)\bigg|_{t=0}

(137)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d}\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\left.\frac{\partial^{n}}{\partial t^{n}}\left[\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}}(t),\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(t))\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}\right]\right|_{t=0}

Since
\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}
are independent of
t
, by applying multivariate Faà di Bruno formula for
\beta^{[2]}
, we have

\frac{\partial^{n}}{\partial t^{n}}\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}}(t),\lambda_{k_{1}})=\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(n)}\frac{n!}{\prod_{j=1}^{n}b_{j}!\cdot j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}}(t),\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{n}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}(t)\right)^{b_{j}},

where
\mathcal{P}(n)
denote the set of all integer partitions
\pi=(b_{1},b_{2},\dots,b_{m})
such that
\sum_{j=1}^{n}j\cdot b_{j}=n
.

Similarly, by applying chain rule of derivative to projectors and nilpotents, we also have

\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\left[(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(t))\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)\right]=\sum_{j=0}^{n}\binom{n}{j}\left(-\frac{d^{j}}{dt^{j}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(t)\right)\cdot\frac{d^{n-j}}{dt^{n-j}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t).

By defining

\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j)}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mbox{\tiny def}}}{{=}}\left.\frac{d^{j}}{dt^{j}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}(t)\right|_{t=0},\quad\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(n-j)}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mbox{\tiny def}}}{{=}}\left.\frac{d^{n-j}}{dt^{n-j}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)\right|_{t=0},

finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}(t)\bigg|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d}\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+\ell=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{\ell!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}

(138)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}\beta^{[2]}\right)(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\cdot\left[\sum_{j=0}^{\ell}\binom{\ell}{j}\left(-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j)}\right)\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(\ell-j)}\right]\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}},

where
\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}:=\left.\frac{d^{j}}{dt^{j}}\lambda_{k_{d}}(t)\right|_{t=0}
.

This formula computes how the generalized interpolation kernel operator
\mathfrak{X}
changes under perturbations of the form
\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}
, taking into account the full Jordan structure and the role of divided differences in the operator function calculus.

The evaluation of
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}}

Since we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}}=

(139)

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([0,0,q_{1}])}}{([0!,0!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}},

to derive the
n
-th derivative of the operator-valued expression

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}},

with respect to
t
based on Eq. (
139
), we must again analyze the impact of differentiating the terms where
\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}
enters implicitly via the spectral data:
\lambda_{k_{d}}(t)
,
\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)
, and
\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)
. Finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,N}}(t)\Big|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+\ell=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{1}!\cdot\ell!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!\cdot j!^{b_{j}}}

(140)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[2]})^{([0,0,q_{1}])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\cdot\left[\sum_{j=0}^{\ell}\binom{\ell}{j}\left(-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j)}\right)\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(\ell-j)}\right]\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}.

The Evaluation of
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}

Since we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}=

(141)

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([0,q_{d},0])}}{([0!,q_{d}!,0!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},0,0])}}{([q_{c}!,0!,0!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{d},0])}}{([q_{d}!,0!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},0])}}{([q_{c}!,0!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}},

to derive the
n
-th derivative of the operator-valued expression

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}(t),

with respect to
t
, based on Eq. (
141
), we have to analyze the impact of differentiating terms where
\bm{X}+t\bm{Y}
enters implicitly via the spectral data:
\lambda_{k_{d}}(t)
,
\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)
, and
\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}(t)
.

By applying same techniques, Faà di Bruno formula and the chain rule of derivatives, as the evaluation of
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{P},\bm{D}_{P},\bm{X}_{1,P}}
, we have the final
n
-th derivative results:

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,P}}(t)\Big|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+j_{1}+j_{2}=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{d}!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\frac{1}{j_{1}!j_{2}!}

(142)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[2]})^{([0,q_{d},0])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\cdot\left(-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j_{1})}\right)\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j_{2})}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+j_{1}+j_{2}=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{c}!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\frac{1}{j_{1}!j_{2}!}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[2]})^{([q_{c},0,0])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\cdot\left(-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j_{1})}\right)\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(j_{2})}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{d},0])}}{q_{d}!}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(n)}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+j_{1}=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{c}!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\frac{1}{j_{1}!}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[1]})^{([q_{c},0])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(j_{1})}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{1},i_{1}}.

Evaluation of
\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}

Since we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}=

(143)

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([0,q_{d},q_{1}])}}{([0!,q_{d}!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[2]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},0,q_{1}])}}{([q_{c}!,0!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}(\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}})\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{d},q_{1}])}}{([q_{d}!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{c},q_{1}])}}{([q_{c}!,q_{1}!])}\times\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}\bm{Y}\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}},

to derive the
n
-th derivative of the operator-valued expression,

\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}(t),

with respect to
t
based on Eq. (
143
), we have the following final result:

\displaystyle\frac{d^{n}}{dt^{n}}\mathfrak{X}^{\bm{C}_{N},\bm{D}_{N},\bm{X}_{1,N}}(t)\Big|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+j_{1}+j_{2}=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{d}!q_{1}!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\frac{1}{j_{1}!j_{2}!}

(144)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[2]})^{([0,q_{d},q_{1}])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\cdot\left(-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j_{1})}\right)\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j_{2})}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+j_{1}+j_{2}=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{c}!q_{1}!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\frac{1}{j_{1}!j_{2}!}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[2]})^{([q_{c},0,q_{1}])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\cdot\left(-\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(j_{1})}\right)\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(j_{2})}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},k_{d},i_{d},q_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\beta^{[1]}(\lambda_{k_{c}},\lambda_{k_{1}}))^{([q_{d},q_{1}])}}{q_{d}!\cdot q_{1}!}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{c},i_{c}}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{q_{d},(n)}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{c},i_{c},q_{c},k_{d},i_{d},\\
k_{1},i_{1},q_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m+j_{1}=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}(m)}\frac{1}{q_{c}!q_{1}!}\cdot\frac{m!}{\prod_{j=1}^{m}b_{j}!j!^{b_{j}}}\cdot\frac{1}{j_{1}!}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\partial_{\lambda_{k_{d}}}^{|\pi|}(\beta^{[1]})^{([q_{c},q_{1}])}\right)(\lambda_{k_{d}},\lambda_{k_{1}})\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{m}\left(\lambda_{k_{d}}^{(j)}\right)^{b_{j}}

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{c},i_{c}}^{q_{c}}\cdot\bm{P}_{k_{d},i_{d}}^{(j_{1})}\cdot\bm{Y}\cdot\bm{N}_{k_{1},i_{1}}^{q_{1}}.
